## 📌 Legend & Tier Badges
- 🟢 **`[GOATED / TOP PICK]`**: Exceptionally lenient evaluation, relaxed attendance/vivas, top GPA yield.
- 🔵 **`[SUPPRESSED GEM / VALUE PICK]`**: Downvoted on VitFac; chat confirms high leniency and low stress.
- 🟡 **`[MODERATE / SAFE BACKUP]`**: Standard predictable grading; great secondary choice.
- 🟣 **`[POLARIZING / CONDITIONAL]`**: High marks possible, but classroom behavior moody or strict.
- 🔴 **`[STRICT TRAP / BLACKLIST]`**: Heavy penalties, ruthless lab checks, gatekept internals.
- ⚠️ **FLAG**: VitFac rating and WhatsApp sentiment significantly contradict each other.
